    10→Work Log:
    11→- Verified stack: Next.js 16.1.3 (Turbopack) running on port 3000, Bun 1.3.14, Node 24.19.0, full shadcn/ui set, framer-motion, sonner, lucide-react present.
    12→- Read Caddyfile: gateway listens on :81; requests with `?XTransformPort={port}` are proxied to `localhost:{port}`; everything else goes to :3000. So the frontend MUST call the backend via relative URLs like `/api/all?XTransformPort=4000`.
    13→- Connectivity tests from sandbox: TMDB 200 (key valid), Jikan 200 (but flaky — rate-limited 504s observed on /random and /top/characters), TVmaze 200, all 4 RSS feeds 200, football-data.org 200 (X-Auth-Token valid, PL standings accessible), jolpi.ca/ergast F1 200, YouTube Data API 200 (key valid).
    14→- nekos.best → 403, api.waifu.pics → unreachable. Decision: SKIP waifu-art endpoints.
    15→- Neon PostgreSQL: TCP+TLS reachable, but authentication FAILS ("password authentication failed for user 'neondbowner'") on pooler AND non-pooler hosts, with and without sslmode/channel_binding params. Credentials from user .env are rejected by Neon. Decision: backend is Neon-first (uses DATABASEURL exactly as provided) with an automatic local JSON-file fallback driver + 60s auto-reconnect probe, so the platform stays fully functional; it will switch to Neon automatically once valid credentials are supplied.
    16→- ESLint config is lenient (no-img-element off, unused vars off) — plain <img> allowed.
    17→- layout.tsx uses Geist fonts; metadata to be updated for Animaxia branding.
    18→
    19→Stage Summary:
    20→- Architecture: Express backend as mini-service at /home/z/my-project/mini-services/animaxia-backend (port 4000, `bun --watch`), Next.js frontend single-route SPA at src/app/page.tsx with hash-based routing (#/home, #/category/:type, #/content/:id, #/player/:id, #/search, #/franchise, #/recommendations, #/admin) because only the / route is user-visible in this environment.
    21→- API contract between backend and frontend defined (see Task 1-a spec).
    22→- Design system: dark theme #0a0a10, rose/pink accent (NO blue/indigo), type badges: anime=rose, movie=amber, tv=teal, news=orange, sports=lime, mined=fuchsia, featured=violet.

   177→Task ID: 6
   178→Agent: main-orchestrator (Z.ai Code)
   179→Task: Fix Neon PostgreSQL connection (user re-sent the exact connection string) — root cause, migration to Postgres, E2E re-verification, ZIP regeneration
   180→
   181→Work Log:
   182→- User re-sent the exact DATABASEURL. Root cause of the previous auth failure found: the backend .env contained a CORRUPTED connection string (`neondbowner:npgAw7mcryLTtV5@.../metadata01` — missing underscores in user/db name, wrong password). Correct string: `neondb_owner:npg_dFuciCvV25wz@.../metadata_01`.
   183→- Verified corrected string directly with a pg script: CONNECTED to metadata_01 as neondb_owner; table `content` already existed but with an INCOMPATIBLE legacy schema (external_id/created_at columns from earlier testing) — deleted the stale empty table (only 2 junk rows: test/player-test).
   184→- Fixed mini-services/animaxia-backend/.env DATABASEURL line (verbatim user string, no `psql` prefix per user's own .env note).
   185→- Improved db.js: extracted `migrateLocalToPostgres()` (idempotent upserts + archives data/content.json → content.json.migrated); now called at BOOT after successful initDb (previously migration only ran via the 60s reconnect probe, so a fresh boot against a working Neon would never adopt local rows). Reconnect probe refactored to reuse the same function.
   186→- Process-management discovery: processes started via Bash tool invocations are tree-killed at invocation end (even with nohup/setsid — they stay in the invocation's process tree). Fix: double-fork orphanization — `( setsid bun run dev > backend.log 2>&1 < /dev/null & )` — the `bun run dev` parent gets PPid=1 (adopted by tini) BEFORE the invocation ends, escaping the kill. Backend now survives across invocations (same state as the original 12:12 instance).
   187→- Backend restarted on :4000: driver postgres (connected: true), 64/64 local rows migrated into Neon, schema created, boot seed skipped (count>0).
   188→- API verification on live Neon: /api/all (64→66 items), /api/content/1, /api/content/1/player (direct mode, 2 sources), POST /api/miner (Wikipedia Attack on Titan + Neon Genesis Evangelion) — both upserts verified DIRECTLY in Neon via SQL (ids 99, 100).
   189→- Regenerated public/animaxia-backend.zip (28 files) with corrected .env + improved db.js; excluded node_modules/backend.log/data runtime artifacts. Verified download: 200, 48,821 bytes.
   190→- Agent-browser E2E through Caddy gateway :81 (isolated session): home renders (hero= freshly mined item, Trending TMDB, Popular anime Jikan); zero page errors; Admin shows "Neon PostgreSQL connected" + 65/66 items + cache/rate-limit stats; Player #/player/1: primary Google CDN mp4 403s from sandbox IP (known env limit, fine in user's browser) — switched to redundant test-videos.co.uk source: readyState 4, duration 10s, paused=false (actually playing); Miner tab UI: mined https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neon_Genesis_Evangelion → extracted title+thumbnail+YouTube embed (1.1MB in 1135ms) → "Open in library" → #/content/100 renders; Search 'frieren' → 1 result from Neon; ZIP download 200; sticky footer verified (1280x900 short 404 page: footerBottom=viewportH=900, stickyOk=true).
   191→
   192→Stage Summary:
   193→- ROOT CAUSE of "Neon auth failed" was a corrupted DATABASEURL in .env (lost underscores + wrong password). Now fixed with the user's exact string; Neon PostgreSQL is the ACTIVE driver (66 items), local fallback retained as safety net.
   194→- New capability: boot-time migration of local-fallback rows into Postgres (self-healing for ZIP users who first run without a valid DB then add one).

   213→- AUTH /api/auth: POST /register {email,username,password} → {ok,user,accessToken,refreshToken}; POST /login {email,password} → same; POST /refresh {refreshToken} → {ok,accessToken,refreshToken}; POST /logout {refreshToken} → {ok}; GET /me (Bearer) → {user}. user shape {id,email,username,avatar,role,plan,planexpiresat,preferences,createdat}.    214→- USERS /api/users (all Bearer): GET /me → {user, stats:{watchlist,history,comments}}; PATCH /me {username?,avatar?,preferences?} → {user}; POST /me/avatar {image(dataURL≤512KB)} → {user}; GET/POST /me/watchlist, DELETE /me/watchlist/:contentId; GET /me/history, POST /me/history {contentId,progress}; GET/PUT /me/preferences {types,genres,franchises}.    215→- PREMIUM /api/premium: GET /plans → {data:[{key,name,price,period,limits,features,color}]}; GET /me → {plan,limits,subscription,usage}; GET /verify → {plan,active,limits}; POST /checkout {plan} → {ok,sessionId,url}; POST /confirm {sessionId} → {ok,user,subscription}; POST /cancel → {ok,subscription}.    216→- SOCIAL /api/social: GET /content/:id/comments → {data:[threads w/ replies, likes, dislikes, myVote, user{username,avatar}]} (optionalAuth); POST /content/:id/comments {body,parentid?} (auth); DELETE /comments/:id (own); POST /comments/:id/vote {value:1|-1|0}; POST /content/:id/like {value:1|-1|0} → {ok,likes,dislikes,myVote}; GET /content/:id/likes; GET /feed/global?cursor= → {data:[{type:comment|like|content|user, createdat, user?, content{ id,title,type,thumbnail }}], nextCursor}; GET /feed/personal (auth) same shape boosted by preferences+history.

Local BM25+fuzzy+prefix engine, dirty-flag auto-resync from content table (server.js emits content:changed via platform/events.js).
   220→- CACHE /api/cache: GET /stats → {lru:{...},global:{...}}; POST /invalidate {tags?:[],prefix?} (admin). modules/cache/advancedCache.js = LRUCache + tags + per-user namespace + event invalidation (content:changed/comment:added/plan:changed/user:changed).
   221→- WORKERS /api/workers: POST /mine {url,type?,externalId?} → {ok,jobId}; POST /prefetch {contentId} → {ok,jobId}; POST /thumbnails {contentId?|missing:true} → {ok,jobId,count?}; POST /refresh {source:tmdb|jikan} → {ok,jobId}; GET /jobs → {data}; GET /jobs/:id → job; GET /status → {pool,jobs,schedules}.    548→- Key architectural findings from the domain routers (documented, not worked around by editing domain/):
   549→  • ai/ai.js: 5 routes (GET /recommendations, POST /summarize, POST /describe, POST /chat, GET /status); chat history table = `aichats` (contentid, userid, role, message, createdat — verified in common/migrations.js DDL); quota model = per-plan daily (10/100/500) + 5/day anonymous IP bucket; SDK lazily created via ZAI.create(), 20s timeout + 1 retry, null → heuristic fallback.
   550→  • analytics/analytics.js: 4 routes (POST /views, GET /trending, GET /user/:id, GET /overview); raw table = `viewevents`; migrations define NO rollup table → worker owns one.
   551→  • search/search.js: 4 routes (GET /advanced BM25+fuzzy, GET /autocomplete, POST /index admin force-rebuild, GET /status); the index is IN-PROCESS memory (inverted index, no Postgres index table, no exported rebuild function); auto-dirty via in-process `content:changed` event — which never fires cross-service in the cloud (content upserts happen in content-service).
   552→  • requireAuth/requireAdmin in domain/users/auth.js verify the Bearer token and set req.user THEMSELVES (no dependency on attachUserFromToken) → search-service can run attachUser:false while POST /api/search/index still guards correctly.
   553→- services/ai-service/ (8 files): package.json (@animaxia/ai-service; express ^4.21.2, dotenv ^16.4.7, z-ai-web-dev-sdk ^0.0.18, @animaxia/common *, @animaxia/domain *); server.js (createService: name ai-service, port 4003, portEnv AIPORT, initDb true, ensureSchema ensurePlatformSchema, attachUser true — quotaGuard + /recommendations personalization read req.user, routers [aiRouter at /api/ai], worker {intervalMs 600000}, health readiness, rateLimit); worker.thread.js (every 10 min: `DELETE FROM aichats WHERE createdat < NOW() - ($1::text)::interval` with 7-day retention, rowCount → detail { deleted, retention }; per-run dynamic import of @animaxia/common/db.js with own pg pool, initDb→task→closeDb, never-overlapping, non-fatal catch → { ok:false, error }; responds to parent 'run'); health.js (db ping only — z-ai deliberately NOT a readiness check since heuristic fallback is a valid serving mode; provider health = GET /api/ai/status); rateLimit.js ({ windowMs 60000, max 60 } — tighter than fleet 120 because LLM is the most expensive path; RATELIMITWINDOW_MS/RATELIMITMAX still override); cache.js (createCache ttlMs 60000 / 2000 entries, deliberately NOT wired in front of the router — front-caching would let anonymous clients farm cached LLM output past the daily quota; domain module caches via advancedCache after the quota guard); Dockerfile (§8 frozen body, ai-service/4003, byte-exact); README.md (real endpoint table from the router, quota/cost model, .z-ai-config SDK setup incl. Docker /etc/.z-ai-config mount note, worker task, env table, gateway curl examples).
   554→- services/analytics-service/ (8 files): server.js (port 4004, portEnv ANALYTICSPORT, attachUser true — POST /views attributes events to req.user; /user/:id + /overview self-guard via requireAuth(+requireAdmin), routers [analyticsRouter at /api/analytics], worker {intervalMs 900000}, rateLimit { 60000, 240 } — generous outer guard because POST /views is the hottest write path and the router already limits it to 120/min per IP); worker.thread.js (every 15 min: idempotent `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dailyviewrollups` — house-style columns contentid/day/views/plays/completed/uniqueviewers/updatedat, PK (contentid, day), FK → content ON DELETE CASCADE, index on day — then `INSERT ... SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE event=...) GROUP BY contentid, date_trunc('day', createdat)::date ON CONFLICT (contentid, day) DO UPDATE` recomputing the trailing window (yesterday+today, LOOKBACK '1 day') so today refreshes every run and late yesterday events fold in; window start resolved with the same DB session timezone via a tiny SELECT; detail { inserted (=upsert rowCount), windowFrom, lookback }); health.js, rateLimit.js, cache.js (60s/2000, not wired — POST /views must never be cached; trending/overview already advancedCache'd), Dockerfile (4004), README.md.
   555→- services/search-service/ (8 files): server.js (port 4005, portEnv SEARCHPORT, attachUser FALSE — search is public and POST /api/search/index self-guards, routers [searchRouter at /api/search], worker {intervalMs 600000}, rateLimit { 60000, 240 } — autocomplete fires per keystroke); worker.thread.js (every 10 min: `SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS docs, MAX(createdat) AS latest FROM content`, compares against the previous run's watermark kept in thread memory — content has no updatedat column so (COUNT, MAX(createdat)) is the best signal — and posts detail { docs, latestContentAt, changed, baseline }; baseline=true on first run after respawn; honestly documented that a worker isolate cannot touch the main thread's in-memory index, so the worker DETECTS change and warms nothing); server.js reindex trigger (polls svc.worker.status().lastTask every 15s — same pattern as the gateway feeding its upstream gauge — and on changed:true emits `content:changed` on the in-process platform event bus via emitPlatform(), the same EventEmitter instance domain/search/search.js subscribed to at import time, marking the index dirty → next query transparently rebuilds under the module's async lock; documented blind spot: metadata-only updates of pre-existing rows move neither COUNT nor MAX(createdat) → backstopped by the daily 04:00 maintenance.reindex queue job and manual POST /api/search/index); cache.js (createCache ttlMs 30000 / 2000 — search results are cache-hot; not mounted by default, documented how to wire cacheMiddleware if needed); health.js, Dockerfile (4005), README.md (incl. "how the index stays fresh" section + status-vs-worker-docs staleness comparison tip).
   556→- Cross-cutting: all three server.js use `import 'dotenv/config'` first, ABSOLUTE worker paths via fileURLToPath(new URL('./worker.thread.js', import.meta.url)) — required because common/worker.js resolves relative paths against common/ itself (gateway 2-b discovery, noted in-code; the task's literal './worker.thread.js' would have spawned common/worker.thread.js) — and mount the domain router at its exact §3 prefix; all worker threads load env via dotenv path anchored to ../../.env, respond to 'run', post heartbeat+task per the common/worker.js contract, and never overlap runs.

  1311→  2. REAL BUG — hub socket never connected for signed-in users whose access token was stale at page load: the socket was created on mount with the pre-refresh token; the realtime service's io.use REJECTS invalid tokens (verified: connect_error 'Invalid or expired access token'), and the stale auth was reused for every reconnection, killing the live ticker + presence for the whole visit (reproduced: sockets stayed 0, no 'online' chip, curl-triggered comments never appeared live). Fix in SocialHub: create the socket only after auth-provider finishes session restore (effect waits on `initializing`, deps [initializing]) so the handshake always carries the fresh post-rotation token; plus a connect_error handler that rotates once via tryRefresh() and re-arms socket.auth for the next attempt (also covers mid-session 15-min token expiry and logout->guest fallback). Same connect_error re-arm added to PartyRoom (its socket already waited for `user`, so only the mid-session expiry case needed covering).
  1312→- QA performed (all through the Caddy gateway :81 — direct :3000 cannot reach :4010 because Next only rewrites /api/*, so socket QA on :3000 is impossible by design):
  1313→  - curl contract verification: register/login throwaway t10five-qa@test.local; people, feed/v2 (auth=follow-graph semantics, public=global), reviews/recent, parties, party detail/join/leave, ai/social/home, party/7 code — all shapes match social.ts types. :4010 health ok; engine.io handshake + raw WebSocket verified through the gateway.
  1314→  - agent-browser end-to-end: #/social hub renders with all 5 tabs; logged-in (ui10testerb via refresh rotation) + anon (guest socket) both verified; presence "N online" chip; LIVE activity events streamed in real time from curl-triggered comments in BOTH auth and anon sessions; People tab search/list/profile dialog (stats, recent reviews/activity); optimistic Follow->Unfollow round-trip; Reviews tab like toggle 0->1->0; Parties tab join-by-code with a real code (JVUFRG) -> room as member ("Synced to host" + Re-sync, no Reconnecting chip, chat message sent via socket AND persisted to the monolith through the bridge — verified id 15 via REST); party room as host (created from content-page button AND from the start-party dialog search flow: "Copy invite code", "You're the host" badge, members crown, Leave ends party — backend also auto-ends a host's previous active party on create, by design); #/content/3 renders Reviews section (posted a review -> aggregate 8.0/10 + count, form switched to Edit/Update prefilled, AI digest button -> summary + sentiment + themes + "based on 1 review") + Watch party button; ?tab= deep link works on fresh loads; 390px mobile viewport sanity check; 0 console errors/warnings on every page after the fixes.

  1418→- Recon data layer: db.js = content (externalid UNIQUE, type, title, metadata JSONB, thumbnail) + query(text, params) public + upsertContent/listContent/getContentById/countContent; modules/platform/sql.js exportă rows/one; LocalStore fallback cu semantici identice.   1419→- Recon workers (pattern de casă pt job-uri, NU se atinge): WorkerPool in-memory pe worker_threads, TASK_FILES mine/prefetch/thumbnail/refresh, FIFO, side effects onSuccess (cache.clearPrefix + emitPlatform('content:changed') + auditLog), scheduler WORKERSREFRESHMS. Miner = sistem DEDICAT DB-backed (persistență Neon), nu worker pool — complementar, nu înlocuitor.
  1420→- Recon providers: tasks/refresh.mjs arată pattern-ul fetch+normalize: fetchProviderJson('tmdb', base + /trending/movie/day, {params:{api_key: TMDBAPIKEY}}) → upsertContent({externalid:'tmdb:movie:{id}', metadata:{source:'tmdb-auto', mediaType, overview, rating, year, genres}}); Jikan: /top/anime → externalid 'jikan:anime:{mal_id}', metadata cu synopsis+score+genres. Env: TMDBAPIKEY/TMDBBASEURL/JIKANBASEURL (fără underscore).
  1421→- Recon tenant (FAZA 11): core.js exportă resolveTenantOf(req), getTenantBySlug/ById, tenantOfUser, recordTenantUsage(tenant, {metric, quantity, unit, meta}), tenantUsageRollup; billing.js are SELECT id, slug, name, plan, status FROM tenants (iterare tenants activi). Vectorembeddings are coloana tenantid (default 'global') + refkeys tenant namespaced 't:slug:kind:refid:0'; global = refkeys 'content:{id}:0' prin indexContent.
  1422→- Recon vector/AI: vector-service.js exportă embedTexts (openai dacă AI_PROVIDER=openai + AI_API_KEY, altfel local-hash-256), contentDocText(row), indexContent({ids}), vectorSearch, indexUser/indexFeed; tenant-vector.js are indexTenantContent (cap 300) — miner va refolosi embedTexts/contentDocText direct pentru indexare granulară per contentIds.
  1423→- Recon ops metrics: MetricsRegistry cu inc/gauge/observe(histograme bounded) + attachPull(ns, fn) pentru /api/metrics collectors + middleware() deja global. Miner: counters animaxia_miner_jobs_total{type,status,tenant}, animaxia_miner_items_upserted_total, histogram animaxia_miner_job_duration_ms, gauges queue depth + attachPull('miner').
  1424→- Recon edge cron: cron/src/index.ts = scheduled handler care face match pe DEFAULT_CONFIG.cron.jobs (shared/config.ts, KV-overridable) + runJob cu CronJobResult în EDGE_CACHE cron:log:*; pattern: endpoint + adminAuth (EDGE_ADMIN_JWT Bearer) + fallbackEndpoint public; body derivat din numele jobului (tmdb-* → {source:'tmdb'}); job-uri existente: daily-picks, weekly-recap, tmdb-refresh, jikan-refresh, cleanup-logs, cleanup-sessions. Gateway worker/src/index.ts are /edge/health, /edge/status (config+bindings), /edge/metrics, /edge/waf/events — miner va adăuga /edge/miner/status.

  1429→- 12-1 Miner data model: modules/miner/miner-migrations.js — ensureMinerSchema idempotent: miner_jobs (id UUID, type, status queued|running|done|failed, tenantid DEFAULT 'global', payload JSONB, result JSONB, error, queuedat/startedat/finishedat, createdby), miner_sources (provider, externalid, contentid, tenantid DEFAULT 'global', lastsync, UNIQUE(provider, externalid, tenantid)), miner_logs (jobid FK, level, message, ts), miner_manifests (contentid, manifesturl, host, tenantid DEFAULT 'global', lastchecked, UNIQUE(contentid, manifesturl, tenantid)) + indexe. Mount în server.js boot după ensureTenantSchema (try/catch degradare, house style).
  1430→- 12-2 Core miner: modules/miner/core.js — scheduleJob(type, tenantid, payload), runJob(jobId), recordSource, upsertManifest, listJobs, jobStats, runner in-proces (maxConcurrent 2, I/O bound fetch+SQL, NU worker_threads), side effects: cache.clearPrefix('http:') + emitPlatform('content:changed') + auditLog + metrics hooks. Providers: modules/miner/providers/tmdb.js (fetchMoviesPage(page), normalizeToContentRow), providers/jikan.js (fetchAnimePage(page), normalizeToContentRow). Mount server.js.
  1431→- 12-3 AI & search: modules/miner/ai-index.js — indexContentForTenant(tenantid, contentIds[]) (embedTexts + contentDocText → INSERT vectorembeddings ON CONFLICT refkey; global → refkeys platform 'content:{id}:0' + tenantid 'global'; tenant → 't:slug:content:{id}:0'), rebuildTenantIndex(tenantid) (bulk cu cap 300). modules/miner/search-index.js — pushToSearchIndex(contentRows, tenantid): dacă OPENSEARCHURL configurat → REST bulk _bulk real; altfel local: catalog compact în advancedCache tag 'miner' + usage event.
